atha daitya-patir nihatān kiṃ karān dṛṣṭvā kautūhalaṃ gatvā asau śrī-kṛṣṇa-pautra iti devarṣiṇā prokto— dhanur ādāya svayam evāniruddhaṃ grahītuṃ tat-samīpam ājagāma ||17|| aniruddho'pi yoddhum āyāntaṃ sahasra-bāhuṃ rājānaṃ dṛṣṭvā tat-parighaṃ bhrāmayitvā bāṇasyopari cikṣepa ||18|| sva-cāpa-nirmuktena bāṇena taṃ parighaṃ ciccheda ||19|| anantaram uragāstreṇa aniruddhaṃ nibiḍaṃ baddhvā svāntaḥ-pure niveśayāmāsa ||20|| atha kṛṣṇo’py evaṃ vidham eva devarṣiṇā jṣātvā baladeva-pradyumna-sahitaḥ sva-senayāvihaṃ gamendram āruhya tasya bāṇasya bhuja-vanaṃ chettum ājagāma ||21|| bali-putreṇa purāśaṅkaro'rcitaḥ prasanno varaṃ vṛṇīṣvety uvāca ||22|| tam īśvaraṃ bāṇo mama pura-dvāri rakṣārthaṃ sarvadopaviśya samāgataṃ para-sainyaṃ jahīty evaṃ varam ayācata ||23|| taṃ tathety uktvā śaṅkaro'pi tasya pura-dvāri sāyudhaḥ sa-putraḥ sa-gaṇaḥ samāsīnas tasminn eva kāle ruṣā-sva-senayā samāgataṃ vāsudevaṃ dṛṣṭvā vṛṣam āruhya sarvāyudhopetaḥ sva-putra-gaṇa-saṃ vṛto yoddhuṃ niścakrāma ||24||
kṛṣṇo'pi taṃ bhūta-patiṃ gajacarmakapālabhasmadharaṃ jvalitoragākalpaṃ piṃ galaṃ trilocanantriśūladharaṃ -
sarva-bhūtagaṇasaṃ hṛtikarttāraṃ sarva-bhūtabhayāvahaṃ saṃ vartāgniprabhaṃ putradvayasamanvitaṃ samastagaṇāvṛtaṃ
prathamaṃ tadabhūdghoraṃ kṛṣṇaśaṅkarayos tadā |

pauṇḍraka-putra-kṛtyāvidhvaṃsanaṃ nāma

atha tatrasthā nanda-gopādayaḥ sarve janāḥ putra-dāra-sahitāḥ paśu-pakṣi-mṛgādayaś ca vāsudeva-prasādena divya-rūpa-dharā vimānam ārūḍhāḥ paramaṃ vaikuṇṭha-lokam avāpuḥ ||28||
kṛṣṇas tu nanda-gopa-vrajaukasāṃ sarveṣāṃ paramaṃ nirāmayaṃ svapadaṃ dattvā divi deva-gaṇaiḥ saṃstūyamāno dvāravatīṃ śrīmatīṃ viveśa ||29||
te tu yudhiṣṭhira-bhīmārjuna-nakula-sahadevāḥ supatnyā draupadyā saha mahāraṇyaṃ gatvā tatra dvādaśābdān sthitvā saṃvatsara-paryantam ajṣātāḥ sarve matsya-deśādhipater virāṭasya niveśane sthitvā vāsudevena sahāyena dhārtarāṣṭrān yoddhum ājagmuḥ ||39||
teṣāṃ dhārtarāṣṭra-pāṇḍu-putrāṇāṃ nānā-deśādhipa-nṛpaiḥ kurukṣetra-mahā-puṇye devānām api bhayaṃ karo mahāsaGgrāmo'bhavat ||40||

kṛṣṇas tam ākrośaṃ śrutvā tuṣṇīm uvāsa ||43||
